### 2.3 03_Bug_Fix.txt

| Lines | Says | Code | Mark | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 4-6 | {F Picks a Minion}: after the main vessel dies and respawns, pressing F selects a controllable minion (Boutman/Boutbro) instead of the main vessel. Repro: play Boutman, let Boutbro spawn, kill, press F after respawn. | `Shared/ModKitFocus.cs:21-54 (Update detects the respawn by a changed hero reference, sets Respawned); Shared/ModKitFocus.cs:56-77 (After: on Focus press, selects the vessel, moves it to the front of the Hero list, calls Select)` | MATCH | Line 4 is the bug heading; 5-6 are the problem and repro steps, which the fix answers. Nothing to build beyond the fix. |
| 7 | Fix: built into the pack's shared code and runs with Argo, Yossele, Spool and Uloe. | `Shared/ModKitFocus.cs:10-18 and 33-35 (Mark('Focusfix') lets one part install the handler per pack; the handler is added to Bl_Inputs.Actions['Focus'])` | MATCH | The shared code is there. The header says every vessel part runs it, but the four vessel parts' own calls to ModKitFocus.Update are not in the files given, so that part is unverified here. |
| 8 | Status: built; the PC pass on a real death and respawn ran with the fix not switched on; fixed since and compiled, so the next PC round checks it again. | `Shared/ModKitFocus.cs:6-9 (header cites kit pitfall #157 and the dead-vessel timing), 10-38 (install and mark)` | MATCH | The fix is built and compiled. The real Boutman death check is still open in PLAN ('the F key on a real Boutman death (P1-1)'), so the status is accurate as stated. |
| 10-11 | {No Way Back In}: a player who loses the connection mid-map has no way back in, because the game shuts the lobby for the whole map. Rejoining mid-map must not interrupt the others. | `09_Rejoin.cs:59-148 (Host: keeps a seat per player who was in the map, sets the door open while a seat is empty, 146-147 and 153-160 Door); 189-202 (TurnAway for someone who was not in the map)` | MATCH | The way back in is built. 'Must not interrupt the others' is only partly shown in code: Restore (177-186) touches only the returning player's Tds_Clientinfo. The code does not prove the lobby reopening leaves the others' play undisturbed; that is a play-test matter. |
| 12 | How to see it: a friend drops out during a map and tries to come back. | `09_Rejoin.cs:550-553 (Closed sets dropped when a non-host client closes mid-map), 515-527 (Watch leaves the map and adds the Join Game line to the error box), 138 (chat line naming the dropped player)` | MATCH | This is the repro for the rejoin path; the code covers it. |
| 13 | Fix: the host keeps each player's seat (vessel, towers, aura) and opens the lobby while a seat is empty; the dropped player comes back with Join Game on the host in Steam. | `09_Rejoin.cs:17-30 (Seat: Hero, Team, Aura, Stats, Info), 59-148 (Host keeps seats, Say at 138 names Join Game), 162-186 (Copy and Restore of vessel and aura), 146-147 and 153-160 (lobby door opened while a seat is empty), 524 (Join Game line in the error box); 10_Online.cs:6-79 (RWIdentity keeps the Sessionid across a restart)` | MATCH | Vessel, aura and the lobby door are kept in the mod's code. Towers are not kept by the pack: 09_Rejoin.cs:4-5 says the game keeps the vessel and towers and gives them back on return (Game_Players.Playerconnected), so towers rest on the game's own handling. Steam's Join Game itself is outside the code. |
| 14 | Status: built, and passed on the PC with a real second player, a game restart included. Steam's Join Game and a real network's drop wait for the online evening. | `09_Rejoin.cs:1-14 (header: the rejoin design); 10_Online.cs:6-79 (RWIdentity, the restart's Sessionid)` | MATCH | The built part matches. The PC pass is recorded in a report, not in code. Steam's Join Game and a real network's drop are the online evening's, as PLAN says. |

| Lines | Says | Code | Mark | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 12:4 | Pointer to the rides and overlap-check reports | `none (reference only)` | MATCH | Documentation pointer with no behaviour of its own; nothing to build. |
| 12:5 | Shop's base-game Cardinals excluded: Moon, Moon Core, Loop, Looped Mirror, Vintage, Same Day Shipping, Pig, Money Bag, Sandbox, Sand, Blurry Photo, Checklist, Computer, Bottom Rock | `17_Cardinals.cs:64-69 NotSold` | MATCH | All 14 names present; Artifact_Rock is Bottom Rock's real id (pitfall #132). The 1% aura cut is the reason for the exclusion, not a built rule. |
| 12:8 | A new Cardinal or Trinket is a run modifier, the same kind of effect as the base game's Cardinals | `17_Cardinals.cs:136-160 Build; 17_Cardinals.cs:632-668 GrantCardinal` | MATCH | Each sheet becomes an Artifactobject with Coreupgrades, granted through Helper_Entitytools.Upgrade. |
| 12:9 | Names: found objects, two plain words each | `17_Cardinals.cs:45-60 Display fields` | MATCH | All sixteen display names are two words. |
| 12:10 | Bought from the shopkeeper with Pure Aura | `18_Shop.cs:217-255 Shelf; 18_Shop.cs:363-374 Buy; 17_Cardinals.cs:547-578 Buy` | MATCH | The shop's cards cost Pure Aura and Buy takes it from p.Pure. |
| 12:11 | Two kinds, Cardinals and Trinkets, kept apart in the shop and in the list | `17_Cardinals.cs:19-22 Trinket flag; 17_Cardinals.cs:349-375 Shelf; 17_Cardinals.cs:96 Sold; 18_Shop.cs:234 kind label` | DIFFERENT | The shop separates them (one trinket slot in Shelf, and a Trinket or Cardinal label on each card). The Sold list holds both kinds in one sorted list (17_Cardinals.cs:161); the split is only by IsTrinket on read. |
| 12:12 | Trinket belongs to its buyer, acts only on their vessel, towers, units or aura, and shows on their vessel's panel | `17_Cardinals.cs:882-900 Grant (panel upgrades on the vessel); 17_Cardinals.cs:975-986 Reach; 24_Trinkets.cs:678-691 Pay` | MATCH | Held per player in p.Trinkets; panel lines go on the holder's vessel; Reach and Coin pay touch only the holder's things. |
| 12:13 | Cardinal acts on the core, the enemies or the clock, is everyone's, and shows in the Cardinal row; every base Cardinal that isn't a Trinket goes on the core | `17_Cardinals.cs:632-668 GrantCardinal (every core, row list up to 8); 17_Cardinals.cs:1060-1098 Row; 17_Cardinals.cs:651 stock Coreupgrades` | MATCH | Cardinals go on every core and into info.Artifacts while it has room under 8. |
